9570               	;
9571               	; 	' REPEATED ADDITION, not a multiply, and deliberately so. Two separate
9572               	; 	' hazards meet on this one line: `tsec * bmul.` is invalid because the `.`
9573               	; 	' suffix marks a 16-bit CONSTANT and both of these are variables, and a
9574               	; 	' plain `tsec * bmul` would be an 8-bit product -- a 50-second capture in
9575               	; 	' the x300 band pays 15,000, which is six times past a byte. Multiplying
9576               	; 	' properly would then land on the MPY/r0 hazard, where a 16-bit var read
9577               	; 	' straight after being multiplied returns the product's HIGH word.
9578               	; 	'
9579               	; 	' At most 50 adds, once per capture. Nothing about this is a hot path.
9580               	; 	'
9581               	; 	' The guard matters: a COMPUTED `FOR 1 TO 0` still runs its body once in
9582               	; 	' CVBasic, so catching Harry on the very last tick would pay a full
9583               	; 	' multiplier instead of nothing.
9584               	; calc_bonus:

10177               	;
10178               	; 	' The font stays in bank 0: it is small, and keeping one readable thing
10179               	; 	' unbanked makes a bank-selection mistake obvious (text survives, art does
10180               	; 	' not) instead of producing a uniformly blank screen.
10181               	; 	INCLUDE "font.bas"
10182               	; 	' Keystone Kapers font -- GENERATED by assets/genfont.py. Do not edit; regenerate.
10183               	; 	'
10184               	; 	' Characters 32-90 (59 of them), 8 bytes each = 472 bytes. Williams' cabinet face:
10185               	; 	' heavy, squared, flat terminals, no serifs -- drawn 5x7 inside the 8x8 cell so a
10186               	; 	' blank column at the right and a blank row at the bottom keep it legible.
10187               	; 	'
10188               	; 	' Loaded with DEFINE CHAR 32,59,font_bits. The run must be CONTIGUOUS, so slots
10189               	; 	' with no glyph emit blanks rather than being skipped -- a gap would shift every
10190               	; 	' letter after it.
10191               	;
10192               	; font_bits:
10193               cvb_FONT_BITS
10194               	; 	DATA BYTE $00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00	' space
10195 EA02 0000     	byte >00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00,>00

10312               	;
10313               	; 	INCLUDE "art.bas"
10314               	; 	' Keystone Kapers art -- GENERATED by assets/genart.py. Do not edit; regenerate.
10315               	; 	'
10316               	; 	' EVERY SPRITE IS 16x16: sprite size and magnification are GLOBAL on the
10317               	; 	' TMS9918, so small objects are drawn small INSIDE a 16x16 box rather than
10318               	; 	' being small sprites.
10319               	; 	'
10320               	; 	' EVERY 8-PX OBJECT SITS IN ROWS 8-15 OF ITS BOX, ducked Kelly included, so
10321               	; 	' one rule places them all: bottom edge = y + 16, and a thing resting on the
10322               	; 	' floor is at y = FLOORY - 16.
10323               	; 	'
10324               	; 	' Sprite bytes are QUADRANT-ORDERED: 16 rows of the left half, then 16 of
10325               	; 	' the right. Every block is an EVEN number of bytes -- an odd DATA BYTE run
10326               	; 	' silently misaligns every word table after it (CLAUDE.md 3A).
10327               	;
10328               	; spr_kelly:	' Kelly RIGHT then LEFT: hat, face, tunic, leg1, leg2, duck-hat, duck-face. Patterns 0..52
10329               cvb_SPR_KELLY
10330               	; 	DATA BYTE $00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00

10593               	;
10594               	; 	' ------------------------------------------------ store characters
10595               	; 	' Codes 96-115. Loaded as ONE contiguous run, so the order here is
10596               	; 	' the order of the codes -- a gap would shift every char after it.

10980               	; 	' -------------------------------------------------- scanner canvas
10981               	; 	' Chars 160-207: 16 cols x 3 rows over screen rows 21-23. Blank to start;
10982               	; 	' dots are plotted by VPOKEing the PATTERN table (rows 21-23 sit in the
10983               	; 	' third screen third, so every scanner pattern is at base 4096).
10984               	;
10985               	; 	' --------------------------------------------------- font colours
10986               	; 	' Chars 32-90, EIGHT bytes each. Without this the font keeps whatever
10987               	; 	' CVBasic left in the colour table, which on a green store made the HUD
10988               	; 	' unreadable.
10989               	;
10990               	; scan_col3:	' one 8-byte block per canvas row: 4 px of BLACK margin, then furniture grey / Kelly black / Harry white / floor line yellow per 4 px level, on dark green
10991               cvb_SCAN_COL3

11061               	; 	INCLUDE "store.bas"
11062               	; 	' Keystone Kapers store -- GENERATED by assets/genstore.py. Do not edit.
11063               	; 	'
11064               	; 	' EIGHT band templates of 5 rows x 32 cols (160 bytes each) plus a 32-byte
11065               	; 	' index over (level, screen). The whole 8-screen, 4-level building is about
11066               	; 	' 1.3 KB instead of the 5,120 bytes it would cost to store literally.
11067               	; 	'
11068               	; 	' All eight templates are ONE contiguous block under ONE label, so a band
11069               	; 	' blit is
11070               	; 	'     SCREEN stor_tpl, tpl*160, toprow*32, 32, 5, 32
11071               	; 	' with no label ladder -- SCREEN needs a literal label and a label cannot be
11072               	; 	' chosen by a variable, which is why RallyX needed a 4-way IF over its maze
11073               	; 	' tables. One block sidesteps it.
11074               	; 	'
11075               	; 	' 160 is EVEN, so this cannot leave the location counter odd and misalign
11076               	; 	' the word tables defined after it.
11077               	; 	'
11078               	; 	' template ids:
11079               	; 	'   0 T_AISLE_A
11080               	; 	'   1 T_AISLE_B
11081               	; 	'   2 T_ESC_W
11082               	; 	'   3 T_ESC_E
11083               	; 	'   4 T_ELEV
11084               	; 	'   5 T_ROOF
11085               	; 	'   6 T_ROOF_W
11086               	; 	'   7 T_ROOF_E
11087               	; 	'   8 T_END_W
11088               	; 	'   9 T_END_E
11089               	;
11090               	; stor_tpl:	' 10 templates x 160 bytes
11091               cvb_STOR_TPL
11092               	; 	DATA BYTE 148,148,148,148,98,148,148,148,148,148,148,98,148,148,148,148
